| 12 | Jesse | H3448 [content]: stepGloss: Jesse — David's father from the tribe of Judah living at the time of United Monarchy, first mentioned at Rut.4.17;; referred to as Jesse (יִשַׁי), or Jesse (Ἰεσσαί);; a son of Obed ;; father of David , Eliab , Abinadab , Shimeah , Nethanel , Raddai , Ozem , Zeruiah , Abigail (?) and Elihu . § son of Boaz and the father of king David |

| 14 | David | H1732 [content]: stepGloss: David — A king of Judah living at the time of United Monarchy, first mentioned at Rut.4.17;; referred to as David or [ ] (KJV, NIV= David) (דָּוִד), or David (Δαυείδ, Δαυίδ, Δαβίδ);; son of Jesse and Nahash ;; a brother of Eliab , Abinadab , Shimeah , Zeruiah , Abigail (?), Nethanel , Raddai , Ozem and Elihu ;; husband of Michal , Abigail , Ahinoam , Maacah , Haggith , Abital , Eglah and Bathsheba ;; father of Amnon , Chileab , Absalom , Adonijah , Shephatiah , Ithream , Shammua , Shobab , Nathan , Solomon , Ibhar , Elishua , Nepheg , Japhia , Elishama , Eliada , Eliphelet , Tamar , Elpelet , Nogah and Jerimoth . § youngest son of Jesse and second king of Israel |
